```json
  "title": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['Title'], triggerBody()?['title'], outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['Email']?['subject'], outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['Email']?['subject'], 'Email task'))}",

  "copyTo": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['CopyTo'], ''))}",
  "priority": "@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['Priority'], triggerBody()?['priority'], 'Normal')}",
  "category": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['Category'], triggerBody()?['category'], ''))}",
  "subCategory": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['SubCategory'], ''))}",
  "categoryCode": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['CategoryCode'], 'CAT'))}",
  "subCategoryCode": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['SubCategoryCode'], 'SUB'))}",
  "ackDue": "@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['AckDue'], outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['AcknolwedgementDueBy'], '')}",
  "taskDue": "@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['TaskDue'], outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['TaskDueDate'], triggerBody()?['dueDate'], '')}",
  "description": "@{trim(coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['Description'], outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['Comments'], triggerBody()?['notes'], ''))}"
}
```

### `Scope_Global/Compose__Standard_Response_Revised`

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|
| Type | `Compose` |
| Kind | — |
| Description | _not declared_ |
| Runs after | `Scope_Finalize_Response_State` (Succeeded/TimedOut/Skipped/Failed) |

**`inputs`, verbatim:**

```json
{
  "ok": "@less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400)",
  "status": {
    "http": "@variables('varStatusCode')",
    "code": "@if(less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400), 'OK', concat('ERR', string(variables('varStatusCode'))))",
    "message": "@if(less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400), 'Success', 'Failed')"
  },
  "request": {
    "requestId": "@variables('varRequestId')",
    "trackingId": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['trackingId']), coalesce(triggerBody()?['trackingID'], ''), triggerBody()?['trackingId'])",
    "action": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['action']), 'emailtotaskassignment', triggerBody()?['action'])",
    "operation": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['operation']), 'create', triggerBody()?['operation'])",
    "mode": "single",
    "requestedBy": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['userEmail']), coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['CreatedBy'], 'unknown'), triggerBody()?['userEmail'])",
    "source": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['source']), 'http', triggerBody()?['source'])"
  },
  "timing": {
    "receivedAtUtc": "@if(empty(variables('varReceivedAtUtc')), variables('varCompletedAtUtc'), variables('varReceivedAtUtc'))",
    "completedAtUtc": "@variables('varCompletedAtUtc')",
    "durationMs": "@variables('varDurationMs')"
  },
  "data": "@variables('varData')",
  "errors": "@variables('varErrors')",
  "meta": {
    "ts": "@variables('varCompletedAtUtc')",
    "runId": "@workflow()?['run']?['name']",
    "flowName": "@workflow()?['name']",
    "contractVersion": "2026-03-23.5"
  }
}
```

font-size:14px; color:#4A4849; line-height:22px;\">\n                  <strong>Recipient:</strong> @{coalesce(outputs('Compose_MatchedDepartmentValue'), 'Designated Officer')}<br>\n                  <strong>Reference:</strong> @{coalesce(outputs('Compose_ReferenceID'), 'TASK-UNSPECIFIED')}\n                </p>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n          </table>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n\n      <tr>\n        <td class=\"mobile-padding\" style=\"padding:0 30px 12px 30px;\">\n          <div style=\"font-size:14px; color:#373435; line-height:23px; margin-bottom:14px;\">\n            You have been assigned a single official task for acknowledgement and action. Use the buttons below to acknowledge receipt or open the linked record.\n          </div>\n\n          <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"background-color:#FAFAF9; border:1px solid #EAF3EF; border-radius:18px;\">\n            <tr>\n              <td style=\"padding:18px 18px 16px 18px;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1.2px; margin-bottom:8px;\">\n                  Email Subject / Record Link\n                </div>\n                <a href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_AttachmentLink'), '#')}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" style=\"display:inline-block; font-size:18px; line-height:26px; color:#05583B; font-weight:800; text-decoration:none;\">\n                  @{coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task_Email')?['body/EmailSubject'], 'Official Task')}\n                </a>\n                <div style=\"margin-top:10px; font-size:12px; line-height:20px; color:#4A4849;\">\n                  Click the subject to open the linked record directly in your browser.\n                </div>\n\n                <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"margin-top:16px;\">\n                  <tr>\n                    <td width=\"33.33%\" class=\"summary-card\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding-right:8px;\">\n                      <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"border:1px solid #E8E6E7; border-radius:14px; background-color:#FFFFFF;\">\n                        <tr>\n                          <td style=\"padding:14px;\">\n                            <div style=\"font-size:10px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px;\">Priority Signal</div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:8px; font-size:13px; line-height:20px; font-weight:800; color:@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Priority_TextColor'), '#1A7250')};\">\n                              @{coalesce(outputs('Compose_PriorityText'), 'Normal')}\n                            </div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:6px; height:6px; border-radius:999px; background-color:@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Priority_BorderColor_Primary'), '#45C578')};\"></div>\n                          </td>\n                        </tr>\n                      </table>\n                    </td>\n                    <td width=\"33.33%\" class=\"summary-card\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding-left:4px; padding-right:4px;\">\n                      <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"border:1px solid #E8E6E7; border-radius:14px; background-color:#FFFFFF;\">\n                        <tr>\n                          <td style=\"padding:14px;\">\n                            <div style=\"font-size:10px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px;\">Current State</div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:8px; font-size:13px; line-height:20px; font-weight:800; color:@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Status_TextColor'), '#373435')};\">\n                              @{coalesce(outputs('Compose_StatusText'), 'Pending Action')}\n                            </div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:6px; height:6px; border-radius:999px; background-color:@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Status_BorderColor_Primary'), '#C4C3C3')};\"></div>\n                          </td>\n                        </tr>\n                      </table>\n                    </td>\n                    <td width=\"33.33%\" class=\"summary-card\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding-left:8px;\">\n                      <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"border:1px solid #E8E6E7; border-radius:14px; background-color:#FFFFFF;\">\n                        <tr>\n                          <td style=\"padding:14px;\">\n                            <div style=\"font-size:10px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px;\">Response Window</div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:8px; font-size:13px; line-height:20px; font-weight:800; color:#0D0C0C;\">\n                              @{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['AckDue'], '—')}\n                            </div>\n                            <div style=\"margin-top:6px; font-size:11px; line-height:18px; color:#5F5C5D;\">\n                              Acknowledge within the stated period.\n                            </div>\n                          </td>\n                        </tr>\n                      </table>\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                </table>\n\n                <table role=\"presentation\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"margin-top:18px;\">\n                  <tr>\n                    <td class=\"btn-full\" align=\"left\">\n                      <!--[if mso]>\n                      <v:roundrect xmlns:v=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:vml\" xmlns:w=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word\" href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Task_Acknowledgement_Link'), '#')}\" style=\"height:46px;v-text-anchor:middle;width:220px;\" arcsize=\"10%\" strokecolor=\"#05583B\" fillcolor=\"#05583B\">\n                      <w:anchorlock/>\n                      <center style=\"color:#FFFFFF;font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:bold;\">Acknowledge Task</center>\n                      </v:roundrect>\n                      <![endif]-->\n                      <!--[if !mso]><!-- -->\n                      <a href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Task_Acknowledgement_Link'), '#')}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" style=\"display:inline-block; background-color:#05583B; color:#FFFFFF; font-size:14px; font-weight:800; padding:14px 22px; border-radius:10px; border:1px solid #05583B;\">\n                        Acknowledge Task\n                      </a>\n                      <!--<![endif]-->\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"btn-gap\" width=\"10\" style=\"width:10px;\">&nbsp;</td>\n                    <td class=\"btn-full\" align=\"left\">\n                      <!--[if mso]>\n                      <v:roundrect xmlns:v=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:vml\" xmlns:w=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word\" href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_AttachmentLink'), '#')}\" style=\"height:46px;v-text-anchor:middle;width:180px;\" arcsize=\"10%\" strokecolor=\"#E8E6E7\" fillcolor=\"#FFFFFF\">\n                      <w:anchorlock/>\n                      <center style=\"color:#373435;font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:bold;\">Open Record</center>\n                      </v:roundrect>\n                      <![endif]-->\n                      <!--[if !mso]><!-- -->\n                      <a href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_AttachmentLink'), '#')}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" style=\"display:inline-block; background-color:#FFFFFF; color:#373435; font-size:14px; font-weight:800; padding:14px 22px; border-radius:10px; border:1px solid #E8E6E7;\">\n                        Open Record\n                      </a>\n                      <!--<![endif]-->\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                </table>\n                \n                <!-- SECURITY BYPASS FALLBACK UX -->\n                <div class=\"button-note\">\n                  If the buttons do not open, copy and paste this link into your browser:<br>\n                  <a href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Task_Acknowledgement_Link'), '#')}\" style=\"color:#05583B; text-decoration:none; word-break:break-all;\">@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Task_Acknowledgement_Link'), '#')}</a>\n                </div>\n\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n          </table>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n\n      <tr>\n        <td class=\"mobile-padding\" style=\"padding:4px 30px 22px 30px;\">\n          <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"border:1px solid #E8E6E7; border-radius:14px; overflow:hidden;\">\n            <tr>\n              <td colspan=\"2\" style=\"padding:14px 18px; background-color:#FAFAF9; border-bottom:1px solid #E6F0F8;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1.1px;\">Task Details</div>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n            <tr>\n              <td width=\"50%\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding:16px 18px; background-color:#FFFFFF; border-right:1px solid #E6F0F8;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; margin-bottom:6px;\">Recipient</div>\n                <div style=\"font-size:13px; line-height:21px; color:#0D0C0C; font-weight:700;\">@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_AssignedToTitle_Value'), 'Designated Officer')}</div>\n              </td>\n              <td width=\"50%\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding:16px 18px; background-color:#FFFFFF;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; margin-bottom:6px;\">Reference ID</div>\n                <div style=\"font-size:13px; line-height:21px; color:#0D0C0C; font-weight:700;\">@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_ReferenceID'), 'UNSPECIFIED')}</div>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n            <tr>\n              <td class=\"meta-stack\" width=\"50%\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding:16px 18px; border-top:1px solid #E6F0F8; border-right:1px solid #E6F0F8; background-color:#FAFAF9;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; margin-bottom:6px;\">Classification</div>\n                <div style=\"font-size:13px; line-height:21px; color:#0D0C0C; font-weight:700;\">@{coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task_Email')?['body/Classification'], 'General')}</div>\n              </td>\n              <td class=\"meta-stack\" width=\"50%\" valign=\"top\" style=\"padding:16px 18px; border-top:1px solid #E6F0F8; background-color:#FAFAF9;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#5F5C5D; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; margin-bottom:6px;\">Start Date</div>\n                <div style=\"font-size:13px; line-height:21px; color:#0D0C0C; font-weight:700;\">@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_Email_Task_Input')?['NewActivityTask']?['StartDate'], '—')}</div>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n          </table>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n\n      <tr>\n        <td class=\"mobile-padding\" style=\"padding:0 30px 30px 30px;\">\n          <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\" style=\"background-color:#FAFAF9; border-left:4px solid #E1A100; border-top:1px solid #E6F0F8; border-right:1px solid #E6F0F8; border-bottom:1px solid #E6F0F8; border-radius:8px;\">\n            <tr>\n              <td style=\"padding:18px 20px;\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:#C8102E; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; margin-bottom:8px;\">\n                  Remarks / Instruction\n                </div>\n                <div style=\"font-size:13px; line-height:23px; color:#373435;\">\n                  \"@{coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task_Email')?['body/Remarks'], 'Please proceed with the necessary action as required.')}\"\n                </div>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n          </table>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n\n      <tr>\n        <td style=\"padding:22px 30px; background-color:#FAFAF9; border-top:1px solid #E6F0F8;\">\n          <table role=\"presentation\" width=\"100%\" cellspacing=\"0\" cellpadding=\"0\" border=\"0\">\n            <tr>\n              <td class=\"stack\" valign=\"top\" align=\"left\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:12px; line-height:20px; color:#4A4849;\">\n                  <strong style=\"color:#0D0C0C;\">Workflow Service</strong><br>\n                  Office of the Director General\n                </div>\n              </td>\n              <td class=\"stack\" valign=\"top\" align=\"right\">\n                <div style=\"font-size:12px; line-height:20px; color:#4A4849; text-align:right;\">\n                  <a href=\"@{coalesce(outputs('Compose_SupportLink'), '#')}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" style=\"color:#05583B; font-weight:700; text-decoration:none;\">Support</a><br>\n                  Official workflow notification\n                </div>\n              </td>\n            </tr>\n          </table>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n\n    </table>\n  </center>\n</body>\n</html>"

```json
{
```
```json
  "parameters": {
```

```json
    "emailMessage/Body": "<p>@{outputs('Compose_Email_Notification')}</p>",

  },
  "host": {
    "apiId": "/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_office365",
    "connectionName": "shared_office365",
    "operationId": "SendEmailV2"
  },
  "authentication": "@parameters('$authentication')"
}
```
